There are forests teeming with life and sandy plains reminiscent of deserts. Spot Flanders' only wolf pack, enjoy tranquillity and space, and have adventures in ten unique nature areas. </div></article></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="layout--rs-two-col layout--simple layout layout-section layout--2-col layout--label-rocketship-2-col layout--content-blocks layout--2-col--content-blocks layout--v-space-top-medium layout--v-space-bottom-medium layout--bg-stretched layout--no-image" id="beersel"><div class="layout__cols layout__cols--2-col layout__cols--reversed layout__cols--content-blocks layout__cols--2-col--content-blocks layout__cols--v-align-middle layout__cols--col-spacing-2x"><div  class="layout-builder__region layout__region layout__sidebar layout__region--2-col layout__sidebar--2-col layout__sidebar--2-col--right layout__col layout__col--2-col layout__sidebar--reversed layout__region--content-blocks layout__region--2-col--content-blocks layout__col--content-blocks layout__col--2-col--content-blocks layout__sidebar--2-col--content-blocks layout__sidebar--2-col--content-blocks--right layout__region--size-1-2 layout__region--col-spacing-last-2x"><div  class="layout__sidebar__row layout__sidebar__row--primary layout__sidebar__row--main layout__sidebar__row--main--primary"><div  id="cb--48143" data-bem="cb_text" class="block block--view-mode-default block--provider-layout-builder block--inline-blockcb-text block--type-cb-text block-layout_builder cb--view-mode--left cb-text--view-mode--left content-block"><div  class="field field--name-field-cb-title field--type-string field--label-hidden field-cb-title"><h2 class="heading">National Park Forests of Brabant </h2></div><div  class="field field--name-field-cb-text field--type-text-long field--label-hidden text-long field-cb-text"><p>National Park Forests of Brabant connects the oldest forests in Flanders. Featuring the forests of Hallerbos, Sonian Forest and Meerdaalwoud, it forms a green lung between the cultural cities of Halle, Brussels and Leuven. The rich history of the landscape unfolds in steep slopes and sunken roads, providing a sanctuary for stag beetles. When the sunlight streams through the crowns of the beech cathedral, you experience the same grandeur that attracted kings and dukes. Impressive sites such as Beersel Castle watch over the majestic natural heritage.&nbsp;</p></div><div  class="field field--buttons field--name-field-cb-button field--type-link field--label-hidden"><a
         target="_blank" class="button button--buttons button--name-field-cb-button button--type-link button--label-hidden"

  Discover the Forests of Brabant

</div></article></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="layout--rs-two-col layout--simple layout layout-section layout--2-col layout--label-rocketship-2-col layout--content-blocks layout--2-col--content-blocks layout--v-space-top-medium layout--v-space-bottom-medium layout--bg-stretched layout--no-image" id="bocagelandschap"><div class="layout__cols layout__cols--2-col layout__cols--content-blocks layout__cols--2-col--content-blocks layout__cols--v-align-middle layout__cols--col-spacing-2x"><div  class="layout-builder__region layout__region layout__sidebar layout__region--2-col layout__sidebar--2-col layout__sidebar--2-col--left layout__col layout__col--2-col layout__region--content-blocks layout__region--2-col--content-blocks layout__col--content-blocks layout__col--2-col--content-blocks layout__sidebar--2-col--content-blocks layout__sidebar--2-col--content-blocks--left layout__region--size-1-2 layout__region--col-spacing-first-2x"><div  class="layout__sidebar__row layout__sidebar__row--primary layout__sidebar__row--main layout__sidebar__row--main--primary"><div  id="cb--48146" data-bem="cb_text" class="block block--view-mode-default block--provider-layout-builder block--inline-blockcb-text block--type-cb-text block-layout_builder cb--view-mode--left cb-text--view-mode--left content-block"><div  class="field field--name-field-cb-title field--type-string field--label-hidden field-cb-title"><h2 class="heading">Boundless Bocageland </h2></div><div  class="field field--name-field-cb-text field--type-text-long field--label-hidden text-long field-cb-text"><p>In Boundless Bocageland, three geographical regions come together: the Flemish Voerstreek, the Walloon Land of Herve and Dutch Heuvelland. The area forms a green heart in the Meuse-Rhine Euroregion. Looking down at the Landscape Park from above, you would see an area just like a patchwork. Sunken roads, rows of trees, hedges and stream valleys all connect the pieces of landscape. It is these linear elements that give the area its name: 'Bocageland'.</p></div><div  class="field field--buttons field--name-field-cb-button field--type-link field--label-hidden"><a
         target="_blank" class="button button--buttons button--name-field-cb-button button--type-link button--label-hidden"

  Discover Boundless Bocageland

Discover rolling hills, vast orchards and charming villages that give the region its personality. Here, you can hike or cycle through ancient landscapes, enjoy seasonal splendour and get a flavour of local craftsmanship. Relax, be inspired and experience the rich nature and culture of this region.</p></div><div  class="field field--buttons field--name-field-cb-button field--type-link field--label-hidden"><a
         target="_blank" class="button button--buttons button--name-field-cb-button button--type-link button--label-hidden"

  Be amazed by this region 
  </a></div></div></div></div><div  class="layout-builder__region layout__region layout__content layout__region--2-col layout__content--2-col layout__content--2-col--left layout__col layout__col--2-col layout__content--reversed layout__region--content-blocks layout__content--content-blocks layout__col--content-blocks layout__col--2-col--content-blocks layout__region--2-col--content-blocks layout__content--2-col--content-blocks layout__content--2-col--content-blocks--left layout__region--size-1-2 layout__region--col-spacing-first-2x"><div  class="layout__content__row layout__content__row--top"><div  id="cb--48148" data-bem="cb_image" class="block block--view-mode-default block--provider-layout-builder block--inline-blockcb-image block--type-cb-image block-layout_builder cb--layout-mode--normal content-block"><div class="content"><div  class="field field--name-field-cb-media field--type-entity-reference field--label-hidden field-cb-media"><article  class="media media--type-bynder media--view-mode-drimage-scale" ><div  class="field field--name-field-kadanza-id field--type-string field--label-hidden field-kadanza-id"><div  class="group"><div  class="group kadanza-wrapper kadanza-lazy-wrapper kadanza-lazy-wrapper--sized" style="--kadanza-ratio: 800 / 533"><div class="lazy-wrapper"><img

class="layout__sidebar__row layout__sidebar__row--primary layout__sidebar__row--main layout__sidebar__row--main--primary"><div  id="cb--48151" data-bem="cb_text" class="block block--view-mode-default block--provider-layout-builder block--inline-blockcb-text block--type-cb-text block-layout_builder cb--view-mode--left cb-text--view-mode--left content-block"><div  class="field field--name-field-cb-title field--type-string field--label-hidden field-cb-title"><h2 class="heading">Hoge Kempen National Park </h2></div><div  class="field field--name-field-cb-text field--type-text-long field--label-hidden text-long field-cb-text"><p>The Hoge Kempen National Park is a robust area with more than 12,000 hectares of outstanding nature. A place to enjoy the most beautiful panorama of Flanders and be surprised by the extraordinary landscapes: purple heather, golden dunes, endless forests and magnificent fens. The slag heaps and pithead hoists are a typical sight on the horizon and recall the mining days of Belgian Limburg. The park gates each tell their own story. Whether you come hiking or cycling, to mountain bike, horse ride or play: in the Hoge Kempen there's something for everyone!&nbsp;</p></div><div  class="field field--buttons field--name-field-cb-button field--type-link field--label-hidden"><a
         target="_blank" class="button button--buttons button--name-field-cb-button button--type-link button--label-hidden"
    href="https://www.nationaalparkhogekempen.be/en"  >

  Discover the Hoge Kempen 
  </a></div></div></div></div><div  class="layout-builder__region layout__region layout__content layout__region--2-col layout__content--2-col layout__content--2-col--right layout__col layout__col--2-col layout__region--content-blocks layout__content--content-blocks layout__col--content-blocks layout__col--2-col--content-blocks layout__region--2-col--content-blocks layout__content--2-col--content-blocks layout__content--2-col--content-blocks--right layout__region--size-1-2 layout__region--col-spacing-last-2x"><div  class="layout__content__row layout__content__row--main"><div  id="cb--48149" data-bem="cb_image" class="block block--view-mode-default block--provider-layout-builder block--inline-blockcb-image block--type-cb-image block-layout_builder cb--layout-mode--normal content-block"><div class="content"><div  class="field field--name-field-cb-media field--type-entity-reference field--label-hidden field-cb-media"><article  class="media media--type-bynder media--view-mode-drimage-scale" ><div  class="field field--name-field-kadanza-id field--type-string field--label-hidden field-kadanza-id"><div  class="group"><div  class="group kadanza-wrapper kadanza-lazy-wrapper kadanza-lazy-wrapper--sized" style="--kadanza-ratio: 800 / 533"><div class="lazy-wrapper"><img

That's the Meuse River. It flows through the landscape like a powerful blue vein. With the Meuse as your guide, you're unlikely to get lost in the valley. In fact, water is always nearby in this Landscape Park. You walk along ancient gullies, castle estates and special heritage spots in the winding string of Meuse villages. On the banks of the river, you will find quiet spots where you can sit down with a picnic basket bursting with goodies.&nbsp;</p></div><div  class="field field--buttons field--name-field-cb-button field--type-link field--label-hidden"><a

                                                Nationaal Park Scheldevallei - Toerisme Scheldeland
                                    
  
</div></article></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="layout--rs-two-col layout--simple layout layout-section layout--2-col layout--label-rocketship-2-col layout--content-blocks layout--2-col--content-blocks layout--v-space-top-medium layout--v-space-bottom-medium layout--bg-stretched layout--no-image" id="vlaamseardennen"><div class="layout__cols layout__cols--2-col layout__cols--reversed layout__cols--content-blocks layout__cols--2-col--content-blocks layout__cols--v-align-middle layout__cols--col-spacing-2x"><div  class="layout-builder__region layout__region layout__sidebar layout__region--2-col layout__sidebar--2-col layout__sidebar--2-col--right layout__col layout__col--2-col layout__sidebar--reversed layout__region--content-blocks layout__region--2-col--content-blocks layout__col--content-blocks layout__col--2-col--content-blocks layout__sidebar--2-col--content-blocks layout__sidebar--2-col--content-blocks--right layout__region--size-1-2 layout__region--col-spacing-last-2x"><div  class="layout__sidebar__row layout__sidebar__row--primary layout__sidebar__row--main layout__sidebar__row--main--primary"><div  id="cb--48156" data-bem="cb_text" class="block block--view-mode-default block--provider-layout-builder block--inline-blockcb-text block--type-cb-text block-layout_builder cb--view-mode--left cb-text--view-mode--left content-block"><div  class="field field--name-field-cb-title field--type-string field--label-hidden field-cb-title"><h2 class="heading">Landscape Park Flemish Ardennes </h2></div><div  class="field field--name-field-cb-text field--type-text-long field--label-hidden text-long field-cb-text"><p>Between the lively cities of Lille, Ghent and Brussels lies a tranquil landscape of rolling hills: Landscape Park Flemish Ardennes. A region that is legendary among cycling enthusiasts, especially for its hills and cobbled lanes. But there's so much more to experience in the park. There are valleys with meandering streams and mills, beech forests that turn delightful shades of copper in the spring and plentiful water sources.&nbsp;</p></div><div  class="field field--buttons field--name-field-cb-button field--type-link field--label-hidden"><a
         target="_blank" class="button button--buttons button--name-field-cb-button button--type-link button--label-hidden"
    href="https://www.rlva.be/landschapspark"  >

  Discover the Flemish Ardennes
  </a></div></div></div></div><div  class="layout-builder__region layout__region layout__content layout__region--2-col layout__content--2-col layout__content--2-col--left layout__col </div></article></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="layout--rs-two-col layout--simple layout layout-section layout--2-col layout--label-rocketship-2-col layout--content-blocks layout--2-col--content-blocks layout--v-space-top-medium layout--v-space-bottom-medium layout--bg-stretched layout--no-image" id="zwin"><div class="layout__cols layout__cols--2-col layout__cols--content-blocks layout__cols--2-col--content-blocks layout__cols--v-align-middle layout__cols--col-spacing-2x"><div  class="layout-builder__region layout__region layout__sidebar layout__region--2-col layout__sidebar--2-col layout__sidebar--2-col--left layout__col layout__col--2-col layout__region--content-blocks layout__region--2-col--content-blocks layout__col--content-blocks layout__col--2-col--content-blocks layout__sidebar--2-col--content-blocks layout__sidebar--2-col--content-blocks--left layout__region--size-1-2 layout__region--col-spacing-first-2x"><div  class="layout__sidebar__row layout__sidebar__row--primary layout__sidebar__row--main layout__sidebar__row--main--primary"><div  id="cb--48159" data-bem="cb_text" class="block block--view-mode-default block--provider-layout-builder block--inline-blockcb-text block--type-cb-text block-layout_builder cb--view-mode--left cb-text--view-mode--left content-block"><div  class="field field--name-field-cb-title field--type-string field--label-hidden field-cb-title"><h2 class="heading">Landscape Park the Zwin Region </h2></div><div  class="field field--name-field-cb-text field--type-text-long field--label-hidden text-long field-cb-text"><p>Discover the Zwin Region: a unique, cross-border coastal landscape where sea, land and humans have been in dialogue for centuries. Be enchanted by salty nature, vast polders, creeks, dikes and the finest culinary experiences. With its silence, tranquillity and unique stories, this region is a hidden gem, in the green outskirts of Bruges and the vast hinterland of the (Zeeland) Flemish coast. &nbsp;It is naturally a dynamic region, where the fresh and the salty, the new and the old, all continue to inspire each other.&nbsp;</p></div><div  class="field field--buttons field--name-field-cb-button field--type-link field--label-hidden"><a
         target="_blank" class="button button--buttons button--name-field-cb-button button--type-link button--label-hidden"
    href="https://www.zwinstreek.eu/en"  >

  Discover the Zwin Region

</div></article></div><div  class="teaser-background__body"><div  class="teaser-background__spacer"></div><div  class="field field--name-field-link field--type-link field--label-hidden field-link"><div class="field__items field__items--name-field-link"><div class="field__item field__item--name-field-link field__item--type-link field__item--label-hidden"><h3><a href="https://www.vlm.be/nl/themas/platteland/landschapskwaliteit%20en%20onderhoud/landschapsparken/Paginas/default.aspx" target="_blank">About Landscape Parks</a></h3></div></div></div><div  class="field field--name-field-description field--type-string-long field--label-hidden field-description">

        
    
                                                A Landscape Park emphasises landscape quality and multifunctionality. A place where recreation, heritage, tourism, nature and agriculture go hand in hand. 
                                    
  
</div></div></div></article></div><div  id="cb--48162" data-bem="teaser_card" class="block block--view-mode-teaser-background block--provider-layout-builder block--inline-blockteaser-card                                                 Bjorn Snelders
                                    
  
</div></article></div><div  class="teaser-background__body"><div  class="teaser-background__spacer"></div><div  class="field field--name-field-link field--type-link field--label-hidden field-link"><div class="field__items field__items--name-field-link"><div class="field__item field__item--name-field-link field__item--type-link field__item--label-hidden"><h3><a href="https://www.natuurenbos.be/nationaleparkenvlaanderen" target="_blank">About National Parks </a></h3></div></div></div><div  class="field field--name-field-description field--type-string-long field--label-hidden field-description">

        
    
                                                In a National Park, nature and biodiversity take centre stage. They are robust parks, with exceptional nature that must be sustainably protected.
                                    
  
</div></div></div></article></div></div></div><div class="layout--rs-one-col layout--simple layout layout-section layout--1-col layout--label-rocketship-1-col layout--content-blocks layout--1-col--content-blocks layout--v-space-top-medium layout--v-space-bottom-minimal layout--bg-stretched"><div  class="layout-builder__region layout__region layout__region--1-col layout__region--content-blocks layout__region--1-col--content-blocks"><div  id="cb--48163" data-bem="cb_text" class="block block--view-mode-default block--provider-layout-builder block--inline-blockcb-text block--type-cb-text block-layout_builder cb--view-mode--left cb-text--view-mode--left content-block"><div  class="field field--name-field-cb-title field--type-string field--label-hidden field-cb-title"><h2 class="heading">Come and discover the Flemish Parks through the 4 seasons</h2></div><div  class="field field--name-field-cb-text field--type-text-long field--label-hidden text-long field-cb-text"><p>A visit to the Flemish Parks is a unique experience every season. One that heightens your senses. In winter, the sound of fresh snow crunching beneath your feet. When spring arrives, the delightful scent of fruit trees in full bloom. During a summer bike ride, the feeling of sunshine on your skin. And when you come in the autumn? Then you will see golden cornfields and the leaves turning yellow, brown and red.&nbsp;</p></div></div></div></div><div class="layout--rs-four-col layout--simple layout layout-section layout--4-col layout--label-rocketship-4-col layout--content-blocks layout--4-col--content-blocks layout--v-space-top-medium layout--v-space-bottom-medium layout--bg-stretched"><div class="layout__cols layout__cols--4-col layout__cols--content-blocks layout__cols--4-col--content-blocks layout__cols--v-align-top layout__cols--col-spacing-1x"><div  class="layout-builder__region layout__region layout__region--4-col layout__col layout__col--4-col layout__region--content-blocks layout__region--4-col--content-blocks layout__col--content-blocks layout__col--4-col--content-blocks layout__region--4-col--01 layout__sidebar--4-col--content-blocks--01 layout__region--col-spacing-first-1x"><div  class="layout__content__row layout__content__row--01 layout__content__row--main layout__content__row--main--01"><div  id="cb--48164" data-bem="cb_text" class="block block--view-mode-default block--provider-layout-builder block--inline-blockcb-text block--type-cb-text block-layout_builder cb--view-mode--left cb-text--view-mode--left content-block"><div  class="field field--name-field-cb-text field--type-text-long field--label-hidden text-long field-cb-text"><div class="cookiehub-embed-placeholder cookiehub-embed-placeholder--has-thumbnail" data-consent="marketing" data-inverse data-thumbnail="https://www.visitflanders.com/sites/visitflanders/files/public/video_thumbnails/_3AopIdN6W0.jpg"><p>Enable marketing cookies to show this content.</p><p><a href="#" class="ch2-open-settings-btn eucc-open">Manage cookie preferences</a></p></div><iframe width="320" height="560" title="Colours of Flanders   Borgloon" frameborder="0" allow="accelerometer;
